# DTZ word frames

Prepared: 1 November 2026. Source: `C:\Users\90505\Documents\POS\german-app\german-exam-obsidian\Generated\Sentence Game\Tables`.

These three documents expand the website’s current selection of 69 words, 19 phrases and 258 sentence entries. They cover the complete **1,300-row original ladder collection**, its **260 seed rows** and **130 frame families**, rather than choosing a top-N subset. They are content documents in the website repository; the public website is unchanged.

The 4,925-row word-frequency and 3,627-row chunk-frequency tables describe a wider extraction corpus. Their counts are corpus observations, not exam probabilities. Raw forms without a contextual example in the original ladder collection are outside this frame bank; they have not been silently turned into teaching content. Original sentence-candidate tables supply metadata and hashes, not published sentence text.

Navigation: [Word frames](word-frames.md) · [Phrase frames](phrase-frames.md) · [Sentence frames](sentence-frames.md).

## Scope and use

**2,062 distinct word forms**, including grammatical words, drawn from all 1,300 original ladder rows. Entries are surface forms: `fährt`, `fahren` and `gefahren` remain separate when observed. This is not a lemmatised dictionary; articles and verb government stay visible in the example instead of being guessed from isolated spelling.

Each row supplies a contextual gap frame and a complete example sentence. Insert the exact displayed form into `[word]`. Case-folding groups sentence-initial and sentence-internal occurrences; the displayed spelling is taken from the chosen example. A different inflection requires a separately checked sentence.

The example is the shortest complete available source sentence for that form. A greeting or closing with no sentence context receives a labelled editorial completion. Level labels belong to the source row, not to the isolated word.
